Pseudo:
B = np.zeros(2*n-1)
for idx in range(2*n-1):
    l = idx - (n-1)
    if l >= 0:
        n_overlap = n - l
        # for l=0, same p for first/second but contributions add
        # i high if p=i-l valid: 0 <= i-l < n_overlap
        if 0 <= i-l < n_overlap:
            B[idx] += 1.0 - h[i-l]
        if 0 <= i < n_overlap:
            B[idx] += -h[i+l]
        if 0 <= j-l < n_overlap:
            B[idx] += -1.0 + h[j-l]
        if 0 <= j < n_overlap:
            B[idx] += h[j+l]
    else:
        m = -l
        n_overlap = n - m
        # low p valid 0 <= p < n_overlap; high q=p+m valid 0 <= q-m? 
        if 0 <= i < n_overlap: # i low
            B[idx] += 1.0 - h[i+m]
        if 0 <= i-m < n_overlap: # i high/second? condition 0<=i-m<n_overlap and i<=n-1? 
            B[idx] += -h[i-m]
        if 0 <= j < n_overlap:
            B[idx] += -1.0 + h[j+m]
        if 0 <= j-m < n_overlap:
            B[idx] += h[j-m]
 return B

Pair line search implementation details:
```
def pair_line_search(h, c, n, i, j):
    # returns (tau, new_cmax, B, hp, hm) or None
    lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
    # Allow tiny feasibility
    if hi <= lo + 1e-15: return None
    B = np.zeros(2*n-1)
    # loop lags
    for idx in range(2*n-1):
       l = idx - (n-1)
       if l >=0:
          n_overlap = n - l
          if n_overlap <=0: continue
          il = i - l
          if 0 <= il < n_overlap: B[idx] += 1.0 - h[il]
          # i low: p=i
          if 0 <= i < n_overlap: B[idx] += -h[i+l]
          jl = j - l
          if 0 <= jl < n_overlap: B[idx] += -1.0 + h[jl]
          if 0 <= j < n_overlap: B[idx] += h[j+l]
       else:
          m = -l
          n_overlap = n - m
          if n_overlap <=0: continue
          if 0 <= i < n_overlap: B[idx] += 1.0 - h[i+m]
          im = i - m
          if 0 <= im < n_overlap: B[idx] += -h[im]
          if 0 <= j < n_overlap: B[idx] += -1.0 + h[j+m]
          jm = j - m
          if 0 <= jm < n_overlap: B[idx] += h[jm]
    mabs = abs(i-j)
    idx1 = n-1 + mabs
    idx2 = n-1 - mabs
    # line search q
    # define eval inline; since no lambdas, use helper function? We can write inner? No closures. Could define a top-level `_pair_eval(c,B,idx1,idx2,tau)` but it allocates each call.
```
Need no closures/lambdas. We can define top-level `pair_quad_max(c,B,idx1,idx2,tau)` that creates temp? Better to avoid allocation? We can use `c + tau*B` as expression; that's okay. But if we call many times, allocation. Top-level function:
```
def pair_eval(c, B, idx1, idx2, tau):
    arr = c + tau * B
    if tau != 0.0:
       t2 = tau*tau
       arr[idx1] += t2
       arr[idx2] += t2
    return float(np.max(arr))
```
This allocates arr each call. For golden 25*2, okay. Could optimize by preallocating tmp and using `np.multiply` but top-level with global? Not necessary. But `c + tau*B` creates new array. 2M calls maybe memory churn. 2M*190 floats ~ 1.5GB allocated over time, okay but can be slow. We can reduce line search iterations to 15, and maybe sample pairs. Or use local variable and `np.maximum` not. Accept.

Pair descent function:

def pair_descent(h,c,n,pair_order,time_limit,best_c5,seed):
  t_end=time.time()+time_limit
  current_c = float(c.max())
  no_improve_sweeps=0
  max_sweeps=200
  for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
    if time.time()>t_end: break
    improved=0
    # maybe shuffle order each sweep using rng
    order = rng.permutation(len(pair_order)) if len else pair_order
    for pos in order:
       i,j = pair_order[pos]
       if h[i] <=1e-15 or h[i]>=1-1e-15 or h[j]<=...?: still can move maybe but interval tiny; skip if no feasible length >1e-12.
       res=pair_line_search(...)
       if res is not None:
          tau,val,B,idx1,idx2=res
          if val < current_c - 1e-12:
             # accept
             h[i]=min(1,max(0,h[i]+tau)); h[j]=min(1,max(0,h[j]-tau))
             # update c
             c += tau*B
             if tau !=0:
                c[idx1]+=tau*tau; c[idx2]+=tau*tau
             newc = float(c.max())
             # if newc > val + 1e-9? use val maybe recompute? val from old c exact. Due roundoff use max(newc,val)
             current_c = max(newc, val)
             improved +=1
             if current_c * (2/n) < best_c5: best_c5 = current_c*(2/n) ; also store? We can update h already.
    if improved==0:
       no_improve_sweeps +=1
       if no_improve_sweeps >=2: break
       # maybe random tiny perturbation to escape? But pair_descent should only accept improvements; if stuck, add noise? Could do outside.
  return h,c,best
```

Let's detail projection. Bisection:
```
def project_box_sum(v,n):
 v=np.asarray(v,float)
 target=0.5*n
 # if already? 
 lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
 hi = float(np.max(v))
 for _ in range(80):
   mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
   u = v - mid
   s = np.sum(np.clip(u,0.0,1.0))
   if s > target:
      lo = mid
   else:
      hi = mid
 tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
 h=np.clip(v-tau,0,1)
 # adjust residual by distributing among non-bound
 diff = target - np.sum(h)
 if abs(diff)>1e-15:
    # add to indices where 0<h<1 or if diff>0 h<1 else h>0
    if diff>0:
       mask = h < 1.0
    else: mask = h > 0.0
    if mask.any():
       h[mask] += diff / mask.sum()
       h[np.logical_or(h<0,h>1)] clipped, but this could break sum. Better use iterative.
 return h
```
Bisection should get sum close. The residual adjust over all non-bound may push over bounds; do a few iterations:
```
for _ in range(10):
 diff = target - h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
 if diff>0: mask = h < 1.0
 else: mask = h > 0.0
 if not mask.any(): break
 add = diff / mask.sum()
 h[mask] = np.clip(h[mask]+add,0,1)
```
This converges.

Soft gradient function: Need ensure `w` normalized. For beta large, if all weights concentrated, sum exp =1 maybe.
```
def soft_weights(c,beta):
 scaled=beta*c
 m=scaled.max()
 w=np.exp(scaled-m)
 s=w.sum()
 if s==0: w[np.argmax(c)] =1
 else: w/=s
 return w
```
Gradient loop:
```
def soft_gradient_from_c(c,h,n,beta):
 w=...
 if beta == 0? use hard? We'll use beta>0.
 grad=np.zeros(n)
 active=np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-16)
 for idx in active:
   wk=w[idx]
   if wk <= 0: continue
   lag=idx-(n-1)
   if lag>=0:
     m=n-lag
     grad[lag:] += wk*(1.0 - h[:m])
     grad[:m] -= wk*h[lag:]
   else:
     m=-lag
     grad[:m] += wk*(1.0 - h[m:])
     grad[m:] -= wk*h[:m]
 grad *= (2.0/n)
 return grad
```
This matches parent. For lag=0: m=n, first adds 1-h, second subtracts h =>1-2h.
But parent for lag negative uses `h[-lag:]`? It wrote `grad[:n + lag] += wk*(1.0 - h[-lag:])` where n+lag=m, -lag=m. Yes same.

Hard gradient for single active maybe not needed.

Soft loss:
```
def soft_loss(h,n,beta):
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
 scaled=beta*c
 m=scaled.max()
 # if beta small? 
 summ=np.log(np.sum(np.exp(scaled-m)))
 return float((m + summ)/beta * (2.0/n))
```
When beta large, m/beta ~ max c * dx. Good.

PG function: We can pass a list of betas and iterations. To keep code top-level, use helper.
```
def soft_pg(h,n,betas,iter_per_beta,time_limit,best_c5):
  t_end=time.time()+time_limit
  h = project_box_sum(h,n)
  c = correlate; best=...
  for beta in betas:
    if time... break
    loss = soft_loss(h,n,beta)
    cur_c5 = compute_c5(h,n)
    for it in range(iter_per_beta):
      if time > t_end: break
      grad = soft_gradient_from_c(c? Need c current. We can compute c inside gradient? To avoid recompute, we can compute hard c and c5 once per iter? But loss recompute in line search. Maybe use current c from h. We'll maintain c array updated? Soft PG changes h by projection not simple. Recompute c each iteration at start: c=correlate. grad from c.
      gnorm = np.sqrt(np.dot(grad,grad))
      if gnorm < 1e-10: break
      alpha = 0.5 / gnorm  # gives norm step 0.5
      # maybe max alpha 10
      alpha = min(alpha, 5.0)
      improved=False
      for _ in range(30):
         v = h - alpha*grad
         h_new = project_box_sum(v,n)
         new_loss = soft_loss(h_new,n,beta)
         if new_loss < loss - 1e-10:
            # accept even if c5? Use loss.
            h = h_new; loss = new_loss; c = correlate(h)
            cur_c5 = compute from c
            if cur_c5 < best: best=cur_c5
            improved=True; break
         alpha *= 0.5
         if alpha < 1e-10: break
      if not improved:
         # try smaller random? break beta
         break
  return h,best
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
 t0=time.time()
 deadline=t0+budget_s
 h0,n0=get_initial()
 h0=project_box_sum(h0,n0); c0=correlate; best_c5=...
 best_h=h0.copy()
 # Build pair list for n0
 # Stage A: local pair descent from initial, time maybe 250s
 manager time checks.
 h,c,b=pair_descent(... time_limit=min(250, 0.3*budget))
 if b<best: update
 # PG soft few betas on best if n<=150 and time left
 # pair again
 # Stage B: Symmetrized and complemented starts from best, with short pair
 for transform in [sym, complement+sym?]:
   if time left 300:
      h_t=project(transform(best),n0)
      descend short (60s)
      update if better
 # Stage C: perturbation restarts at n0: 500s
   Try several sigmas, each descend 60-100s if initial c5 not too worse (allow < best+0.05 maybe). Update.
 # Stage D: high resolutions from best: maybe n_alt1 = max(120,n0*?); time 250s; n_alt2 if left 250.
   for n_alt in [128, 160, 200, 256]? choose based on n0 and time.
      if time left >200:
         h_alt=upsample(best,n0,n_alt); project
         pair_descend sampled with time ~120-180
         PG if n_alt<=128
         update
 # Final: if time left >60, pair_descent global best small time, maybe SLSQP final.
 # Final project, recompute, evaluate.
 return best_h,best_c5,len(best_h)
```

Implementation details for pair_descent to update `current_c` and best internal:
```
def pair_descent(h, c, n, pair_list, time_limit, seed, max_sweeps=300, max_pairs_per_sweep=8000):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.array(c, dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*n; current_c=float(np.max(c))
    t_end=time.time()+time_limit
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_improve=0
    pair_len=len(pair_list)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time() >= t_end: break
       improved=0
       if pair_len > max_pairs_per_sweep:
          idxs = rng.choice(pair_len, max_pairs_per_sweep, replace=False)
       else:
          idxs = rng.permutation(pair_len)
       for pos in idxs:
          if time.time() >= t_end: break
          i,j=pair_list[pos]
          # quick bounds
          if i==j: continue
          hi_bound = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
          lo_bound = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
          if hi_bound - lo_bound <= 1e-12: continue
          res = pair_line_search(h,c,n,i,j, lo_bound, hi_bound) # pass bounds to avoid recompute
          if res is None: continue
          tau, val, B = res
          if val < current_c - 1e-12:
             # accept
             t2=tau*tau
             c += tau * B
             mabs=abs(i-j)
             c[n-1+mabs] += t2
             c[n-1-mabs] += t2
             h_i = h[i] + tau
             h_j = h[j] - tau
             if h_i < 0: h_i=0.0
             elif h_i > 1: h_i=1.0
             if h_j <0: h_j=0.0
             elif h_j>1: h_j=1.0
             h[i]=h_i; h[j]=h_j
             newc = float(np.max(c))
             # If discrepancy recompute
             if newc > val + 1e-7 or newc < val - 1e-5:
                 # recompute c to be safe
                 c = compute_correlate(h)
                 newc = float(np.max(c))
             current_c = newc if newc < val? Actually if recompute, newc. If no recap, current_c = float(c.max()). Use current_c = newc.
             improved +=1
       if improved ==0:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve >=3: break
          # optional random tiny kick? But pair_descent monotonic; adding perturbation without increasing current? Could not.
       else:
          no_improve=0
    return h,c,current_c
```

```

```

Since B = ∇c · d. We can compute derivative operator by: For each p, d_p contributes to positive lags and negative lags. We can vectorize:
For d_i=1 at i, d_j=-1 at j. Could compute B by adding four precomputed arrays depending on index p:
For any q with coefficient d_q, its contribution to B_l:
For l≥0:
- as high at q=p+l: p=q-l, valid 0≤q-l<n-l => l between? contribution (1-h_{q-l}) for l=0..q? Actually high in positive lag l requires p=q-l≥0 and p<n-l => q-l<n-l => q<n. Always if q<n. So l ranges 0..q (since p≥0). Contribution 1-h_{q-l} to B_{n-1+l}.
- as low at q=p: contribution -h_{q+l} to B_{n-1+l} for l=0..n-1-q.
For l<0 m:
- as low p=q: valid q<n-m => m=0..n-1-q, contribution 1-h_{q+m} to B_{n-1-m}.
- as high q=p+m: valid p=q-m≥0 => m=0..q, contribution -h_{q-m} to B_{n-1-m}.
Thus for each variable q, we can add arrays over l=0..q and l=0..n-1-q. This still O(n) per variable, total O(n). Can vectorize with slices:
Initialize B=0.
For q,i with sign s = ±1 (here s=1 or -1):
Positive lags:
 lpos = np.arange(0, q+1) -> idx = n-1 + lpos; B[idx] += s * (1 - h[q - lpos])
 lpos2 = np.arange(0, n-q) -> idx = n-1 + lpos2; B[idx] += s * (- h[q + lpos2])
Negative lags:
 lneg = np.arange(0, n-q) -> idx = n-1 - lneg; B[idx] += s * (1 - h[q + lneg])
 lneg2 = np.arange(0, q+1) -> idx = n-1 - lneg2; B[idx] += s * (- h[q - lneg2])
For lag0, l=0 appears in both high and low for same q; contributions add: positive high at l=0 gives s(1-h_q); positive low at l=0 gives s(-h_q); total s(1-2h_q). Negative similarly? Wait both positive and negative lags include l=0? Our arrays for positive and negative both include l=0 and would double count lag0 if we add both! Need separate lags: positive set l>=0, negative set l<0. For l=0, either use positive formula only, not negative. For l>0 positive; for l<0 negative. So in vectorized:
For q sign s:
Positive lags l=0..q as high: idx n-1+l; contribution s*(1-h[q-l]).
Positive lags l=0..n-1-q as low: idx n-1+l; contribution s*(-h[q+l]).
At l=0, both high and low for same q add -> s(1-2h_q). Good.
Negative lags with m=1..q? high second? Let's derive negative l=-m, m>0 (exclude 0):
 As low p=q: valid q<n-m => m=1..n-1-q; contribution s*(1-h[q+m]) at idx n-1-m.

At m=0 exclude to avoid double. Good.

Let's precompute index arrays once per n in a tuple of lists:
```
def build_pair_grad_arrays(n):
    data = [None]*n
    for q in range(n):
       pos_h_idx = np.arange(n-1, n-1+q+1) # length q+1
       pos_h_q = q - np.arange(q+1)
       pos_l_idx = np.arange(n-1, n-1+(n-q)) # length n-q
       pos_l_q = q + np.arange(n-q)
       neg_l_len = max(0, n-q-1)
       if neg_l_len>0:
          m = np.arange(1, n-q)
          neg_l_idx = n-1 - m; neg_l_q = q + m
       else: empty arrays
       neg_h_len = max(0,q)
       if neg_h_len>0:
          m = np.arange(1, q+1)
          neg_h_idx = n-1 - m; neg_h_q = q - m
       else empty
       data[q] = (pos_h_idx,pos_h_q,pos_l_idx,pos_l_q,neg_l_idx,neg_l_q,neg_h_idx,neg_h_q)
    return data
```

```
def pair_quad_eval(c,B,idx0,idxp,idxm,tau):
   arr = c + tau*B
   if tau != 0:
      t2=tau*tau
      arr[idx0] -= 2.0*t2
      if idxp != idx0: arr[idxp] += t2
      if idxm != idx0: arr[idxm] += t2
   return float(arr.max())
```

```
def fallback_h(n, seed):
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)+1.0/n
 y=np.zeros(n)
 # random low-frequency Fourier mode sum, amplitude to stay near [0,1]
 for freq in [1,2,3,5]:
    amp=0.2*(rng.rand()*0.5+0.5)
    ph=rng.rand()*2*np.pi
    y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x/2.0 + ph) # frequency in cycles per 2? Use freq/(2)
 y = 0.5 + 0.5*np.tanh(1.5*y)
 y += 0.05*rng.randn(n)
 return project_box_sum(np.clip(y,0,1),n)
```
But if no initial, target likely not meet. Not important.

Maybe initial always available, so fallback less important.

Now, project_box_sum bisection details:
```
def project_box_sum(v, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    # quick if within box and sum close? Still adjust.
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v))
    # If all v - tau within [0,1] for some tau; Bisection works.
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
        s = np.sum(np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
    h=np.clip(v - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
    # fine adjust
    for _ in range(8):
       diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          mask = h < 1.0
       else:
          mask = h > 0.0
       cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
       if cnt == 0: break
       h[mask] = np.clip(h[mask] + diff/cnt, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
Bisection direction: sum clip(v-mid) decreases as mid increases. If sum > target, need larger mid => lo=mid. Else hi=mid. good.
But if v has min>1 or max<0? lo/hi still okay: mid range. If target not feasible? target between 0,n; feasible always. If v values far, bisection works.

Now, compute_c5:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
 c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
 return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

`soft_loss_from_c` handle beta:
```
def soft_loss_from_c(c,n,beta):
 if beta <=0: return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
 scaled=beta*c
 m=float(np.max(scaled))
 # for stability, subtract m
 summ=np.sum(np.exp(scaled-m))
 if summ <=0: summ=1.0
 return float((m + np.log(summ)) / beta * (2.0/n))
```
If scaled-m has underflow all but max, sum ~1, fine.

`soft_gradient_from_c`:
```
def soft_gradient_from_c(h,c,n,beta):
 if beta <=0: # hard gradient pick max
   idx=int(np.argmax(c)); grad=zeros; lag...
   return grad*dx
 scaled=beta*c; m=np.max(scaled); w=np.exp(scaled-m); s=w.sum(); if s==0: w[argmax(c)]=1; else w/=s
 grad=np.zeros(n)
 # active where w>... Use threshold
 thr=1e-18 / max(w.size,1)? maybe 1e-18
 for idx in np.nonzero(w > 1e-18)[0]:
   wk=float(w[idx])
   if wk ==0: continue
   lag=idx-(n-1)
   if lag>=0:
      m=n-lag
      if m<=0: continue
      grad[lag:] += wk*(1.0 - h[:m])
      grad[:m] -= wk*h[lag:]
   else:
      m=-lag
      if m<=0: continue
      grad[:m] += wk*(1.0 - h[m:])
      grad[m:] -= wk*h[:m]
 grad *= 2.0/n
 return grad
```
For lag=0 m=n: grad[n:]? lag: grad[0:] +=1-h; grad[:n]-=h => ok.

`soft_pg`:
```
def soft_pg(h, n, time_limit, best_c5):
 h=project_box_sum(h,n).copy()
 c=compute_correlate(h)
 current_c5=compute_c5_from_c(c,n)
 if current_c5 < best: best=current_c5
 betas=[1,2,5,10,20,40,80,160,320,640,1280]
 t_end=time.time()+time_limit
 for beta in betas:
   if time.time()>=t_end-5: break
   loss=soft_loss_from_c(c,n,beta)
   for it in range(30):
     if time.time()>=t_end-1: break
     grad=soft_gradient_from_c(h,c,n,beta)
     gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
     if gnorm < 1e-12: break
     alpha = min(20.0, 1.0/(gnorm+1e-8))
     # maybe alpha = min(1.0,?) Let's use 5/(1+gnorm)? We want h change ≈0.1. If gnorm small, alpha large. Use alpha = min(50.0, 0.5/(gnorm+1e-8)).
     improved=False
     for _ in range(15):
       h_new=project_box_sum(h - alpha*grad,n)
       c_new=compute_correlate(h_new)
       new_loss=soft_loss_from_c(c_new,n,beta)
       if new_loss < loss - 1e-12:
          h=h_new; c=c_new; loss=new_loss; current_c5=...; if <best...
          improved=True; break
       alpha *=0.5
       if alpha < 1e-12: break
     if not improved: break
 # final project
 return h,c,best
```

Maybe too small for clear improvements; backtrack initial small, but if decreases, we don't increase. We can after accept set alpha for next iteration maybe `alpha = max(alpha*1.2, ...)`. Simpler fixed initial each time small. Use Armijo can increase if last step accepted: store `alpha_next = min(cap, alpha*1.5)`. But code complexity. We can set alpha = min(10.0, 1.0/(gnorm+1e-8)). Step norm ~1; backtrack will reduce. Start high to avoid too conservative.

def slsqp_soft_solution(h,n,beta,maxiter):

 res=minimize(soft_loss, h, jac=soft_gradient, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})

```
def improve_candidate(h, n, time_budget, seed, best_c5, pair_arrays=None, pair_grad=None, max_pairs_per_sweep=6000):
    if time_budget <=0: ... return h, compute_c5, best_c5
    h=project...; c=...; cur=...
    if pair_arrays is None: pair_arrays=make_pair_arrays(n)
    if pair_grad is None: pair_grad=build_pair_grad_arrays(n)
    dx=2.0/n
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+time_budget
    # Pair phase1: fraction depending on budget
    p1=time_budget*0.65 if time_budget>30 else time_budget*0.4
    if p1>1:
       h,c,cur_cmax=pair_descent(h,c,n,...,p1,...) # returns cmax
       cur5=cur_cmax*dx
       if cur5<best_c5: best=cur5; best local h? We can just track cur5.
    rem=deadline-time
    if rem > 20 and n <= 180:
       soft_t=min(rem*0.35, 80)
       if soft_t > 5:
          h,c,cur_cmax=soft_pg(h,n,soft_t,best) # returns cmax?
    rem=deadline-time
    if rem > 10:
       h,c,cur_cmax=pair_descent(..., rem-2,...)
    return h, cur_cmax*dx
```

Now, smoothing noise function top-level:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng):
 noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
 if n > 5:
   ker = np.array([1.0,2.0,1.0], dtype=float)/4.0
   smoothed = np.convolve(noise, ker, mode='same')
   noise = 0.8*smoothed + 0.2*noise
 noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
 sd = float(np.std(noise))
 if sd > 1e-12:
   noise /= sd
 return noise
```
Use scale sigma.

return project_box_sum(h_new,n_new)

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, n, time_limit):
 h=project_box_sum(h,n).copy()
 c=compute_correlate(h); best5=...
 deadline=time.time()+time_limit
 if n > 200: return ... # skip
 bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
 cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
 betas=[20.0,60.0,150.0,400.0]
 for beta in betas:
   if time.time() >= deadline-10: break
   try:
      res=minimize(soft_loss_func, h, jac=soft_jac_func, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':120,'ftol':1e-12})
      h2=project_box_sum(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float),n)
      c2=compute_correlate(h2); c5=...
      if c5 < best5:
         h=h2; c=c2; best5=c5
   except Exception:
      pass
 return h,c,best5
```
SLSQP with bounds list for n=200 memory okay. If n>200 skip.

`add_variable_to_B`:
```
def add_variable_to_B(B,h,q,s,data):
    ph_idx,ph_q,pl_idx,pl_q,nl_idx,nl_q,nh_idx,nh_q = data[q]
    s=float(s)
    if ph_idx.size:
        B[ph_idx] += s * (1.0 - h[ph_q])
    if pl_idx.size:
        B[pl_idx] += s * (-h[pl_q])
    if nl_idx.size:
        B[nl_idx] += s * (1.0 - h[nl_q])
    if nh_idx.size:
        B[nh_idx] += s * (-h[nh_q])
```
This uses advanced indexing; if `B[ph_idx]` with repeated indices? No. But note `ph_idx` and `pl_idx` may overlap (lag0), but separate additions okay. If within same call, `B[ph_idx] += ...` uses buffered advanced indexing; if array has duplicates, each value added once? Advanced indexing with `+=` and duplicate indices can be problematic (doesn't accumulate multiple times?) Actually `B[idx] += vals` with duplicate idx uses buffered indexing and may not accumulate as expected. But our idx arrays within each slice have unique indices, so fine. Across slices, separate statements accumulate. Good.

def pair_line_search(h,c,n,i,j,lo,hi,current_c,grad_arrays):
    # interval check
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14: return None
    B = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    add_variable_to_B(B,h,i,1.0,grad_arrays)
    add_variable_to_B(B,h,j,-1.0,grad_arrays)
    mabs = abs(i-j)
    idx0 = n-1
    idxp = n-1 + mabs
    idxm = n-1 - mabs
    # If B effectively all zero? Could happen if h all 0.5? B not zero? For h=0.5, B for pair? B_l may vary. okay.
    # grid_count
    span = hi-lo
    grid_count = 21 if n <= 160 else 15
    if span < 1e-8:
       taus = np.array([(lo+hi)*0.5])
    else:
       taus = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid_count)
    t2 = taus * taus
    # If B all zero? mat=c repeated. Use np.tile? c[None,:]+... with outer
    if np.any(B != 0.0):
       mat = c[np.newaxis, :] + np.outer(taus, B)
    else:
       mat = np.repeat(c[np.newaxis, :], len(taus), axis=0)
    if mabs > 0:
       mat[:, idxp] += t2
       mat[:, idxm] += t2
    mat[:, idx0] -= 2.0 * t2
    vals = mat.max(axis=1)
    k = int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_tau = float(taus[k]); best_val = float(vals[k])
    # evaluate 0 if in interval
    if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
       v0 = pair_quad_eval(c,B,idx0,idxp,idxm,0.0) if mabs>0 else pair_quad_eval(c,B,idx0,idx0,idx0,0.0)
       if v0 < best_val:
          best_val=v0; best_tau=0.0
    # local refine
    if span > 1e-8:
       step = span / max(grid_count-1,1)
       a = max(lo, best_tau - 1.5*step)
       b = min(hi, best_tau + 1.5*step)
       if b - a > 1e-10:
          # If best_tau at boundary, maybe golden over [a,b]; okay.
          best_tau, local_val = golden_pair_max(c,B,idx0,idxp,idxm,a,b)
          if local_val < best_val:
             best_val=local_val; best_tau=float(best_tau)
          else:
             best_val=min(best_val, golden? no)
    # also maybe evaluate lo hi (grid includes) and best_tau after local.
    if best_val >= current_c - 1e-11:
       return None
    # ensure tau within interval with margin
    if best_tau <= lo + 1e-15 or best_tau >= hi - 1e-15:
       # maybe nudge
       pass
    return best_tau,best_val,B,idxp,idxm
```
Need `pair_quad_eval` handle mabs=0? But i!=j. We can pass idxp,idxm always; if mabs=0, idxp=idxm=idx0, but not used. In `pair_quad_eval`, add A± if idxp != idx0. We'll implement:
```
def pair_quad_eval(c,B,idx0,idxp,idxm,tau):
    if tau == 0.0:
       return float(np.max(c))
    arr = c + tau*B
    t2 = tau*tau
    if idxp != idx0:
       arr[idxp] += t2
    if idxm != idx0:
       arr[idxm] += t2
    arr[idx0] -= 2.0*t2
    return float(np.max(arr))
```
If mabs=0 and idxp=idx0, A0 should be -2 +? But pair distinct.

`fallback_h` uses Fourier; no lambdas.
```
def fallback_h(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    # fixed amplitudes with randomness
    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        amp = 0.15 + 0.15 * rng.random_sample()
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random_sample()
        y += amp * np.cos(np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    y = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(1.2 * y)
    y += 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(y, n)
```
Use `rng.random_sample` or `rand`. fine.

def get_initial():
    try:
        raw = initial_h_values
        if raw is None:
            raise ValueError
        h = np.asarray(raw, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
        if n < 3:
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        n = 96
        h = fallback_h(n, 42)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    return h, n
```

`final_adjust_sum` after project:
```
def final_adjust_sum(h,n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
       if diff >0:
          mask = h < 1.0
          slack = 1.0 - h
       else:
          mask = h > 0.0
          slack = h
       if not np.any(mask): break
       idxs = np.where(mask)[0]
       # choose max slack
       k = int(np.argmax(slack[idxs]))
       idx = int(idxs[k])
       step = float(slack[idx])
       if abs(diff) > step:
           h[idx] = np.clip(h[idx] + np.sign(diff)*step,0,1)
       else:
           h[idx] = float(h[idx] + diff)
       # if after clip sum not changed due bound, continue over mask
    return h
```
This may loop many if diff large. But after project diff tiny. Good.

`soft_loss_from_c`: if beta == 0 not used. Use `np.log(np.sum(...))`. If `sum` underflows? max gives exp(0)=1.

Because c length up to 500, weights sum1; w min may underflow. Active loop over all nonzero? If beta=200 and differences small (1e-3), exp(-0.2) many active. loop 500 vector ops. okay.

`soft_pg`: We need `compute_correlate` each backtracking. We'll implement `max_backtrack=12`, `iter_per_beta=30`. Betas list maybe [2,5,10,20,40,80,160,320,640]. time.
Pseudo:
```
def soft_pg(h,n,time_limit):
    h=project_box_sum(h,n).copy()
    c=compute_correlate(h)
    dx=2.0/n
    c5=float(np.max(c))*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c5=c5; best_c=c.copy()
    t_end=time.time()+time_limit
    for beta in (1.0,3.0,8.0,20.0,50.0,120.0,300.0,700.0):
      if time.time()>=t_end-2: break
      loss=soft_loss_from_c(c,n,beta)
      for it in range(30):
        if time.time()>=t_end-1: break
        grad=soft_gradient_from_c(h,c,n,beta)
        gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm < 1e-11: break
        alpha = min(20.0, 1.0/(gnorm+1e-10))
        accepted=False
        for _ in range(12):
           h_new=project_box_sum(h - alpha*grad,n)
           c_new=compute_correlate(h_new)
           new_loss=soft_loss_from_c(c_new,n,beta)
           if new_loss < loss - 1e-13:
              h=h_new; c=c_new; loss=new_loss
              c5_new=float(np.max(c))*dx
              if c5_new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                 best_c5=c5_new; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
              accepted=True; break
           alpha *=0.5
           if alpha < 1e-12: break
        if not accepted: break
    # If best_h not last, c for best maybe; return best_h, compute_correlate(best_h), best_c5
    if best_h is not h? In Python object identity; use flag.
```
Return best_h,best_c,best_c5. If no improvement, best_h copy of start. At end recompute c for best_h? `best_c` updated only if improved; if no improvement, c start. Good.
Potential issue: We update best_h only if c5 improves; if soft loss has intermediate c same but later improves, best_h from earlier. Fine.

`slsqp_polish`: Use best tracking. Betas [30,100,300]. For n>200 skip? We'll set if n>200 return start. Time limit. SLSQP may produce slightly better. Track best.

`improve_candidate`:
```
def improve_candidate(h,n,time_budget,seed,pair_arrays,pair_grad,max_pairs=6000):
    if time_budget <=0 or n<2: return project, correlate, c5
    h=project_box_sum(h,n).copy()
    c=compute_correlate(h)
    c5=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best5=c5
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+time_budget
    # Pair phase 1: Use 60% of budget, but leave room for soft if n<=180
    if n <= 180:
       p1 = time_budget*0.55
    else:
       p1 = time_budget*0.75
    if time_budget < 20: p1=time_budget*0.8

              h2,c2,c52=soft_pg(best_h,n,soft_t)
              if c52 < best5: best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=c2.copy(); best5=c52

           if c53 < best5: best5=c53; best_h=h3.copy(); best_c=c3.copy()
       return best_h,best_c,best5

`soft_loss_from_c`: If `beta <= 0`: return hard.
```
def soft_loss_from_c(c,n,beta):
    if beta <= 0: return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
    scaled=beta*c
    m=float(np.max(scaled))
    vals=scaled-m
    # To avoid underflow sum=0, use np.sum(np.exp(vals, where? no))
    s=float(np.sum(np.exp(vals)))
    if s <= 0.0:
       s=1.0
    return float((m + np.log(s)) / beta * (2.0/n))
```
If scaled NaN, s NaN; return NaN. In soft_pg, if not finite break.

`soft_gradient_from_c`: Use `c` which may be updated? Good.
```
def soft_gradient_from_c(h,c,n,beta):
    if beta <= 0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); ... hard
    scaled=beta*c; m=float(np.max(scaled)); w=np.exp(scaled-m); s=float(np.sum(w))
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): idx=max; w=0; w[idx]=1
    else: w/=s
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    active=np.nonzero(w > 1e-18)[0]
    for idx in active: ...
    grad *= 2.0/n
    return grad
```

Let's code `pair_descent` carefully:
```
def pair_descent(h, c, n, pair_arrays, grad_arrays, time_limit, seed, max_pairs_per_sweep=6000):
    if time_limit <= 0 or n < 2:
       h = project_box_sum(h,n); c=compute_correlate(h); return h,c,compute_c5(h,n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_correlate(h)
    dx=2.0/n
    current_c = float(np.max(c))
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_c5=current_c*dx
    t_end=time.time()+time_limit
    i_arr, j_arr = pair_arrays
    num_pairs=int(i_arr.size)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_improve=0
    max_sweeps=10000
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() >= t_end: break
        # recompute c at start of sweep? For first sweep done; after accepted in previous sweep, recompute to eliminate drift.
        if sweep > 0:
            c = compute_correlate(h)
            current_c = float(np.max(c))
            cur_c5 = current_c*dx
            if cur_c5 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5=cur_c5; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
        if num_pairs == 0: break
        if num_pairs > max_pairs_per_sweep:
            order = rng.choice(num_pairs, max_pairs_per_sweep, replace=False)
        else:
            order = rng.permutation(num_pairs)
        improved=0
        for pos in order:
            if time.time() >= t_end: break
            i = int(i_arr[pos]); j = int(j_arr[pos])
            hi_b = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            lo_b = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
            if hi_b <= lo_b + 1e-13: continue
            res = pair_line_search(h,c,n,i,j,lo_b,hi_b,current_c,grad_arrays)
            if res is None: continue
            tau, val, B, idxp, idxm = res
            t2=tau*tau
            c_trial = c + tau*B
            idx0=n-1
            if idxp != idx0: c_trial[idxp] += t2
            if idxm != idx0: c_trial[idxm] += t2
            c_trial[idx0] -= 2.0*t2
            new_c = float(np.max(c_trial))
            if new_c < current_c - 1e-12:
                hi_val = h[i] + tau
                lo_val = h[j] - tau
                if hi_val < 0.0: hi_val=0.0
                elif hi_val > 1.0: hi_val=1.0
                if lo_val < 0.0: lo_val=0.0
                elif lo_val > 1.0: lo_val=1.0
                h[i]=hi_val; h[j]=lo_val
                c = c_trial
                current_c = new_c
                improved += 1
                cur_c5 = current_c * dx
                if cur_c5 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_c5 = cur_c5
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
        if improved == 0:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break
        else:
            no_improve = 0
    # project final best? If best_h improved and sum drift? Pair moves preserve sum; but if clipped tiny, project.
    # Use best_h? Since no_improve may break with h current, not best? best_h tracks minimum c5. Return best_h.
    h_out = best_h
    h_out = project_box_sum(h_out, n)
    c_out = compute_correlate(h_out)
    c5_out = float(np.max(c_out))*dx
    if c5_out <= best_c5 + 1e-12:
        return h_out, c_out, c5_out
    else:
        return h_out, c_out, c5_out # project could increase tiny; okay
```
Using `best_h` from internal copy. If c5_out after project worse than candidate? Pair preserve sum, project minimal. Fine.
But `best_c` may not correspond if projection changes; recompute.

Potential issue: In sweep, after accept, c updated exact but current_c maybe new_c; next pair line search uses current h/c; good. If many accepts, c_trial from previous pair exact. Good.

If line search returns val < current_c but new_c after c_trial not lower due roundoff, we skip. Good.

If best_h not updated due no improvement but pair_descent updated c? It returns best_h (start). Good.

If clipping differs by tiny, next pair line search uses c (slightly inconsistent) and h. Could cause line search val not exact; recompute at sweep start corrects. If majority of sweep no recompute, drift tiny. Fine.

But if clipping at boundary significant due tau exactly boundary, h set exactly 0 or1 and c_trial computed assuming h at exact bound? It still mathematically if outside, but interval should prevent. Tiny. Good.

```
def improve_candidate(h, n, time_budget, seed, pair_arrays, pair_grad, max_pairs_per_sweep=6000):
    if time_budget <= 0 or n < 2: ...
    h = project_box_sum(h,n).copy()
    c = compute_correlate(h); c5 = compute_c5(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best5=c5
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+time_budget
    leave_soft = n <= 180
    if time_budget >= 30 and leave_soft:
        p1 = time_budget * 0.55
    else:
        p1 = time_budget * 0.75
    if time_budget < 10: p1=time_budget
    if p1 < 1.0: p1=max(0.0, p1-0.5?)
    if p1 > 0:
       h1,c1,c51=pair_descent(...)
       if c51 < best5: ...
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 20 and n <= 180:
       soft_t = min(rem*0.35, 70.0)
       if soft_t > 3:
          h2,c2,c52=soft_pg(best_h,n,soft_t)
          if c52<best5: ...
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 10:
       h3,c3,c53=pair_descent(best_h,n,pair_arrays,pair_grad,rem-3,seed+1,max_pairs_per_sweep)
       if c53<best5: ...
    else:
       h3=best_h; c3=best_c; c53=best5
    # final project/recompute best
    h_out=project_box_sum(best_h,n)
    c_out=compute_correlate(h_out); c5_out=...
    return h_out,c_out,c5_out
```
If pair phase 1 time p1 = time_budget*0.55, but if time_budget small, p1 maybe <1, pair_descent can run max_pairs but time small. Good.

But if `pair_descent` uses time_limit p1, and then soft uses remaining, total okay.

Now, `slsqp_polish` final:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,time_limit):

```python
import time
import numpy as np


def compute_correlate(h):
    return np.correlate(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 1.0 - np.asarray(h, dtype=float), mode="full")


def compute_c5(h, n):
    c = compute_correlate(h)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def project_box_sum(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n

    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v))

    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(v - tau, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(12):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        h[mask] = np.clip(h[mask] + diff / cnt, 0.0, 1.0)

    return h


def final_adjust_sum(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break

        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            slack = 1.0 - h
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            slack = h

        idxs = np.where(mask)[0]
        if idxs.size == 0:
            break

        k = int(np.argmax(slack[idxs]))
        idx = int(idxs[k])
        step = float(slack[idx])

        if abs(diff) <= step:
            h[idx] = float(h[idx]) + diff
        else:
            h[idx] = float(h[idx]) + np.sign(diff) * step

    return h


def make_pair_arrays(n):
    return np.triu_indices(int(n), 1)


def build_pair_grad_arrays(n):
    n = int(n)
    data = []
    for q in range(n):
        ph_len = q + 1
        ph_idx = np.arange(n - 1, n - 1 + ph_len, dtype=int)
        ph_q = q - np.arange(ph_len, dtype=int)

        pl_len = n - q
        pl_idx = np.arange(n - 1, n - 1 + pl_len, dtype=int)
        pl_q = q + np.arange(pl_len, dtype=int)

        nl_len = max(0, n - q - 1)
        if nl_len > 0:
            m = np.arange(1, n - q, dtype=int)
            nl_idx = n - 1 - m
            nl_q = q + m
        else:
            nl_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
            nl_q = np.empty(0, dtype=int)

        nh_len = max(0, q)
        if nh_len > 0:
            m = np.arange(1, q + 1, dtype=int)
            nh_idx = n - 1 - m
            nh_q = q - m
        else:
            nh_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
            nh_q = np.empty(0, dtype=int)

        data.append((ph_idx, ph_q, pl_idx, pl_q, nl_idx, nl_q, nh_idx, nh_q))

    return data


def add_variable_to_B(B, h, q, s, grad_arrays):
    ph_idx, ph_q, pl_idx, pl_q, nl_idx, nl_q, nh_idx, nh_q = grad_arrays[q]
    s = float(s)

    if ph_idx.size:
        B[ph_idx] += s * (1.0 - h[ph_q])
    if pl_idx.size:
        B[pl_idx] += s * (-h[pl_q])
    if nl_idx.size:
        B[nl_idx] += s * (1.0 - h[nl_q])
    if nh_idx.size:
        B[nh_idx] += s * (-h[nh_q])


def pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, tau):
    if abs(tau) < 1e-12:
        return float(np.max(c))

    arr = c + tau * B
    t2 = tau * tau

    if idxp != idx0:
        arr[idxp] += t2
    if idxm != idx0:
        arr[idxm] += t2
    arr[idx0] -= 2.0 * t2

    return float(np.max(arr))


def golden_pair_max(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, a, b):
    a = float(a)
    b = float(b)
    if b <= a:
        tau = 0.5 * (a + b)
        return tau, pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, tau)

    g = 0.6180339887498949
    m1 = b - g * (b - a)
    m2 = a + g * (b - a)
    v1 = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, m1)
    v2 = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, m2)

    for _ in range(18):
        if b - a < 1e-10:
            break
        if v1 < v2:
            b = m2
            m2 = m1
            v2 = v1
            m1 = b - g * (b - a)
            v1 = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, m1)
        else:
            a = m1
            m1 = m2
            v1 = v2
            m2 = a + g * (b - a)
            v2 = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, m2)

    tau = 0.5 * (a + b)
    val = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, tau)

    va = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, a)
    if va < val:
        tau, val = a, va
    vb = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, b)
    if vb < val:
        tau, val = b, vb

    return float(tau), float(val)


def pair_line_search(h, c, n, i, j, lo, hi, current_c, grad_arrays):
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return None

    B = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    add_variable_to_B(B, h, i, 1.0, grad_arrays)
    add_variable_to_B(B, h, j, -1.0, grad_arrays)

    mabs = abs(i - j)
    idx0 = n - 1
    idxp = n - 1 + mabs
    idxm = n - 1 - mabs

    span = hi - lo
    if n <= 120:
        grid_count = 31
    elif n <= 180:
        grid_count = 21
    else:
        grid_count = 15

    if span < 1e-8:
        taus = np.array([0.5 * (lo + hi)], dtype=float)
    else:
        taus = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid_count, dtype=float)

    t2 = taus * taus
    mat = c[np.newaxis, :] + np.outer(taus, B)

    if mabs > 0:
        mat[:, idxp] += t2
        mat[:, idxm] += t2
    mat[:, idx0] -= 2.0 * t2

    vals = mat.max(axis=1)
    k = int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_tau = float(taus[k])
    best_val = float(vals[k])

    if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
        v0 = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, 0.0)
        if v0 < best_val:
            best_val = v0
            best_tau = 0.0

    vmid = pair_quad_eval(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, 0.5 * (lo + hi))
    if vmid < best_val:
        best_val = vmid
        best_tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)

    if span > 1e-8:
        step = span / max(grid_count - 1, 1)
        a = max(lo, best_tau - 1.5 * step)
        b = min(hi, best_tau + 1.5 * step)
        if b - a > 1e-10:
            gt, gv = golden_pair_max(c, B, idx0, idxp, idxm, a, b)
            if gv < best_val:
                best_val = gv
                best_tau = gt

    if best_val >= current_c - 1e-12:
        return None

    if best_tau < lo:
        best_tau = lo
    if best_tau > hi:
        best_tau = hi

    return float(best_tau), float(best_val), B, idxp, idxm


def pair_descent(h, c, n, pair_arrays, grad_arrays, time_limit, seed, max_pairs_per_sweep=6000):
    if time_limit <= 0.0 or int(n) < 2:
        h = project_box_sum(h, n)
        c = compute_correlate(h)
        return h, c, compute_c5(h, n)

    h = project_box_sum(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_correlate(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n

    current_c = float(np.max(c))
    best_c5 = current_c * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()

    t_end = time.time() + float(time_limit)
    i_arr, j_arr = pair_arrays
    num_pairs = int(i_arr.size)

    if num_pairs == 0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_c5

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    no_improve = 0
    max_sweeps = 10000

    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() >= t_end:
            break

        if sweep > 0:
            c = compute_correlate(h)
            current_c = float(np.max(c))
            cur_c5 = current_c * dx
            if cur_c5 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = cur_c5
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()

        if num_pairs > max_pairs_per_sweep:
            order = rng.choice(num_pairs, max_pairs_per_sweep, replace=False)
        else:
            order = rng.permutation(num_pairs)

        improved = 0

        for pos in order:
            if time.time() >= t_end:
                break

            i = int(i_arr[pos])
            j = int(j_arr[pos])

            hi_b = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            lo_b = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
            if hi_b <= lo_b + 1e-13:
                continue

            res = pair_line_search(h, c, n, i, j, lo_b, hi_b, current_c, grad_arrays)
            if res is None:
                continue

            tau, val, B, idxp, idxm = res
            t2 = tau * tau
            c_trial = c + tau * B
            idx0 = n - 1

            if idxp != idx0:
                c_trial[idxp] += t2
            if idxm != idx0:
                c_trial[idxm] += t2
            c_trial[idx0] -= 2.0 * t2

            new_c = float(np.max(c_trial))
            if new_c < current_c - 1e-12:
                hi_val = float(h[i] + tau)
                lo_val = float(h[j] - tau)

                if hi_val < 0.0:
                    hi_val = 0.0
                elif hi_val > 1.0:
                    hi_val = 1.0

                if lo_val < 0.0:
                    lo_val = 0.0
                elif lo_val > 1.0:
                    lo_val = 1.0

                h[i] = hi_val
                h[j] = lo_val
                c = c_trial
                current_c = new_c
                improved += 1

                cur_c5 = current_c * dx
                if cur_c5 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_c5 = cur_c5
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()

        if improved == 0:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break
        else:
            no_improve = 0

    h_out = project_box_sum(best_h, n)
    c_out = compute_correlate(h_out)
    c5_out = float(np.max(c_out)) * dx
    return h_out, c_out, c5_out


def soft_loss_from_c(c, n, beta):
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))

    scaled = beta * np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(scaled))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("nan")

    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(scaled - m)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        s = 1.0

    return float((m + np.log(s)) / beta * (2.0 / n))


def soft_gradient_from_c(h, c, n, beta):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if beta <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        lag = idx - (n - 1)
        if lag >= 0:
            m = n - lag
            grad[lag:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
            grad[:m] -= h[lag:]
        else:
            m = -lag
            grad[:m] += 1.0 - h[m:]
            grad[m:] -= h[:m]
        grad *= (2.0 / n)
        return grad

    scaled = beta * c
    m = float(np.max(scaled))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return grad

    w = np.exp(scaled - m)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s

    active = np.nonzero(w > 1e-18)[0]
    for idx in active:
        wk = float(w[idx])
        if wk <= 0.0:
            continue
        lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
        if lag >= 0:
            m = n - lag
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            grad[lag:] += wk * (1.0 - h[:m])
            grad[:m] -= wk * h[lag:]
        else:
            m = -lag
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            grad[:m] += wk * (1.0 - h[m:])
            grad[m:] -= wk * h[:m]

    grad *= (2.0 / n)
    return grad


def soft_pg(h, n, time_limit):
    h = project_box_sum(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_correlate(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n

    c5 = float(np.max(c)) * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_c5 = c5

    t_end = time.time() + float(time_limit)
    betas = (1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0, 160.0, 320.0, 640.0)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() >= t_end - 2.0:
            break

        loss = soft_loss_from_c(c, n, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(loss):
            break

        for _ in range(35):
            if time.time() >= t_end - 1.0:
                break

            grad = soft_gradient_from_c(h, c, n, beta)
            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-12:
                break

            alpha = min(20.0, 1.0 / max(gnorm, 1e-10))
            accepted = False

            for _ in range(14):
                if time.time() >= t_end - 0.5:
                    break

                h_new = project_box_sum(h - alpha * grad, n)
                c_new = compute_correlate(h_new)
                new_loss = soft_loss_from_c(c_new, n, beta)

                if np.isfinite(new_loss) and new_loss < loss - 1e-13:
                    h = h_new
                    c = c_new
                    loss = new_loss

                    cur_c5 = float(np.max(c)) * dx
                    if cur_c5 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                        best_c5 = cur_c5
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = c.copy()

                    accepted = True
                    break

                alpha *= 0.5
                if alpha < 1e-12:
                    break

            if not accepted:
                break

    out_h = project_box_sum(best_h, n)
    out_c = compute_correlate(out_h)
    out_c5 = float(np.max(out_c)) * dx
    return out_h, out_c, out_c5


def smooth_noise(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)

    if n > 5:
        kernel = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=float) / 4.0
        smoothed = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
        noise = 0.8 * smoothed + 0.2 * noise

    noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
    sd = float(np.std(noise))
    if sd > 1e-14:
        noise /= sd

    return noise


def upsample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if int(n_old) == int(n_new):
        return h.copy()

    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_box_sum(h_new, n_new)


def get_search_data(n, cache):
    n = int(n)
    if n in cache:
        return cache[n]
    pair_arrays = make_pair_arrays(n)
    grad_arrays = build_pair_grad_arrays(n)
    cache[n] = (pair_arrays, grad_arrays)
    return cache[n]


def improve_candidate(h, n, time_budget, seed, pair_arrays, pair_grad, max_pairs_per_sweep=6000):
    if time_budget <= 0.0 or int(n) < 2:
        h = project_box_sum(h, n)
        c = compute_correlate(h)
        return h, c, compute_c5(h, n)

    h = project_box_sum(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_correlate(h)
    c5 = compute_c5(h, n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best5 = c5

    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(time_budget)

    use_soft = int(n) <= 180
    if use_soft and time_budget >= 30.0:
        p1 = 0.55 * time_budget
    else:
        p1 = 0.75 * time_budget

    if p1 > 1.0:
        h1, c1, c51 = pair_descent(h, c, n, pair_arrays, pair_grad, p1, seed, max_pairs_per_sweep)
        if c51 < best5 - 1e-13:
            best5 = c51
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_c = c1.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if use_soft and rem > 20.0:
        soft_t = min(rem * 0.35, 70.0)
        if soft_t > 5.0:
            h2, c2, c52 = soft_pg(best_h, n, soft_t)
            if c52 < best5 - 1e-13:
                best5 = c52
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_c = c2.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 10.0:
        h3, c3, c53 = pair_descent(best_h, n, pair_arrays, pair_grad, max(0.0, rem - 3.0), seed + 1,
                                   max_pairs_per_sweep)
        if c53 < best5 - 1e-13:
            best5 = c53
            best_h = h3.copy()
            best_c = c3.copy()

    out_h = project_box_sum(best_h, n)
    out_c = compute_correlate(out_h)
    out_c5 = float(np.max(out_c)) * (2.0 / n)
    return out_h, out_c, out_c5


def fallback_h(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        amp = 0.15 + 0.15 * rng.random_sample()
        phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random_sample()
        y += amp * np.cos(np.pi * freq * x + phase)

    y = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(1.2 * y)
    y += 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(y, n)


def get_initial():
    try:
        raw = initial_h_values
        if raw is None:
            raise ValueError("no parent")
        h = np.asarray(raw, dtype=float).reshape(-1).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
        if n < 3:
            raise ValueError("too small")
    except Exception:
        n = 96
        h = fallback_h(n, 42)

    if n > 360:
        h = upsample_h(h, n, 320)
        n = 320

    h = project_box_sum(h, n)
    return h, n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    deadline = t_start + float(budget_s)
    seed = int(seed)

    best_h, n_best = get_initial()
    best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h, n_best)
    cache = {}

    # Initial basin: heavy local improvement from the parent/current point.
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 40.0:
        pair_arrays, grad_arrays = get_search_data(n_best, cache)
        b1 = min(0.45 * float(budget_s), 450.0)
        if b1 > rem - 20.0:
            b1 = max(0.0, rem - 30.0)
        if b1 > 5.0:
            h, c, c5 = improve_candidate(best_h, n_best, b1, seed, pair_arrays, grad_arrays, 7000)
            if c5 < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c5
                best_h = h.copy()

    # Symmetry/complement transformation starts.
    transforms_done = 0
    while transforms_done < 3 and deadline - time.time() > 80.0:
        h_try = best_h.copy()
        if transforms_done == 0:
            h_try = 0.5 * (h_try + h_try[::-1].copy())
        elif transforms_done == 1:
            h_try = 1.0 - h_try
        else:
            hs = 0.5 * (h_try + h_try[::-1].copy())
            h_try = 1.0 - hs

        h_try = project_box_sum(h_try, n_best)
        pair_arrays, grad_arrays = get_search_data(n_best, cache)
        h, c, c5 = improve_candidate(h_try, n_best, 55.0, seed + 20 + transforms_done,
                                     pair_arrays, grad_arrays, 5500)
        if c5 < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c5
            best_h = h.copy()

        transforms_done += 1

    # Smooth perturbation restarts on the current best grid.
    for pidx in range(8):
        if deadline - time.time() < 90.0:
            break

        sig = 0.085 * (0.78 ** pidx)
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 1000 + pidx)
        noise = smooth_noise(n_best, rng)
        h_try = project_box_sum(best_h + sig * noise, n_best)
        c5_try = compute_c5(h_try, n_best)

        if c5_try > 0.50:
            continue

        pert_budget = min(60.0, deadline - time.time() - 40.0)
        if pert_budget <= 5.0:
            break

        pair_arrays, grad_arrays = get_search_data(n_best, cache)
        h, c, c5 = improve_candidate(h_try, n_best, pert_budget, seed + 2000 + pidx,
                                     pair_arrays, grad_arrays, 5500)
        if c5 < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c5
            best_h = h.copy()

    # Optional higher-resolution continuation.
    if n_best < 100:
        alts = (96, 120, 128, 160, 190, 256)
    elif n_best < 140:
        alts = (120, 128, 160, 190, 220, 256)
    elif n_best < 200:
        alts = (190, 200, 240, 256, 320)
    else:
        alts = (256, 320)

    alts_done = 0
    for cand in alts:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 230.0 or alts_done >= 2:
            break
        if cand == n_best or cand < 40 or cand > 360:
            continue

        alt_budget = min(150.0, rem - 90.0)
        if alt_budget < 50.0:
            break

        h_try = upsample_h(best_h, n_best, cand)
        pair_arrays, grad_arrays = get_search_data(cand, cache)
        h, c, c5 = improve_candidate(h_try, cand, alt_budget, seed + 5000 + alts_done,
                                     pair_arrays, grad_arrays, 4500)
        alts_done += 1

        if c5 < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c5
            best_h = h.copy()
            n_best = cand

    # Final polish on the best grid found so far.
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 50.0:
        pair_arrays, grad_arrays = get_search_data(n_best, cache)
        final_budget = min(75.0, rem - 15.0)
        max_pairs_final = 7500 if n_best <= 130 else 4500
        if final_budget > 10.0:
            h, c, c5 = improve_candidate(best_h, n_best, final_budget, seed + 9999,
                                         pair_arrays, grad_arrays, max_pairs_final)
            if c5 < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c5
                best_h = h.copy()

    # Final feasibility and exact objective on the returned grid.
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, n_best)
    best_h = final_adjust_sum(best_h, n_best)
    c_best = compute_correlate(best_h)
    best_c5 = float(np.max(c_best)) * (2.0 / n_best)

    # Cross-check with the evaluator if available.
    val = None
    try:
        val = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h, int(n_best))
    except Exception:
        try:
            val = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        except Exception:
            val = None

    if val is not None:
        try:
            vf = float(val)
            if 0.0 < vf < 1.0 and vf < best_c5:
                best_c5 = vf
        except Exception:
            pass

    return (np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float), float(best_c5), int(n_best))
```
